- **github-orchestrator:correctness-reviewer**: Use this agent as the correctness lens of the review board. It hunts logic errors, null paths, off-by-ones, race conditions, and swallowed errors, and reports nothing it cannot demonstrate with a concrete failing input. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/correctness-reviewer.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:dedup-detective**: Use this agent to detect duplicate issues semantically — matching on symptom, stack trace, and affected surface rather than title wording — and to search closed issues, where a previously-closed duplicate is itself a signal. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dedup-detective.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:dependency-steward**: Use this agent to report dependency health and drive upgrades — batching by risk so each pull request has a single failure hypothesis, keeping the lockfile in sync, and flagging unmaintained or suspicious packages. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dependency-steward.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:docs-reviewer**: Use this agent as the documentation lens of the review board. It finds docs the diff silently invalidated, undocumented breaking changes, and missing changelog entries — it does not ask for comments on obvious code. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/docs-reviewer.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:dora-analyst**: Use this agent to compute deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, and time to restore from real repository history, decomposed so that every regression points to a specific stage and cause. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dora-analyst.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:epic-decomposer**: Use this agent to decompose a large issue or goal into sub-issues that each deliver standalone value, are sized under the review budget, and are linked and ordered by dependency. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/epic-decomposer.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:flake-detective**: Use this agent to confirm whether a test is genuinely flaky by correlating results across runs, identify the specific source of nondeterminism, and quarantine it behind a tracked issue rather than silently skipping it. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/flake-detective.md) · ★21)
- **github-orchestrator:gh-advisor**: Use this agent to get prioritized, evidence-backed recommendations on what to do next across pull requests, CI, issues, security alerts, and releases — which workflow to launch and which agents to deploy. - **github-orchestrator:gh-orchestrator**: Use this agent to run a full GitHub delivery loop end to end — it decomposes the goal, dispatches the delivery, review, CI, and release teams, holds the blackboard, enforces merge policy, and does not report success until the change is merged and green. - **github-orchestrator:hotspot-scout**: Use this agent to identify change-failure hotspots — files that change often and are often followed by a fix or revert — and diagnose the structural reason each one is fragile. - **github-orchestrator:issue-triager**: Use this agent to triage issues — classifying type and severity from evidence in the report, identifying what is missing for reproduction, estimating size, and routing to an owner. - **github-orchestrator:merge-marshal**: Use this agent as the last step before any merge. - **github-orchestrator:ownership-mapper**: Use this agent to find ownership drift, uncovered and orphaned CODEOWNERS paths, and bus-factor-one modules, and to route reviewers on a pull request by ownership, recent authorship, and current load. - **github-orchestrator:performance-reviewer**: Use this agent as the performance lens of the review board. - **github-orchestrator:pr-author**: Use this agent to author or refresh a pull request title and body. - **github-orchestrator:release-conductor**: Use this agent to run a release — computing the version bump from commit history, verifying every readiness gate against live state, and tagging and publishing only when all of them pass. - **glossary**: - **可信回答**：由 Core 呈现的问题绑定到同宿主、呈现之后、唯一且未消费的用户事件；原生表单回答在同一次调用内以同一 interaction 落账。 - **执行授权依据**：决定是否允许进入实现的稳定语义摘要，包含 scope、执行相关任务/RU、文件范围、恢复语义和当前阻断风险，不包含 review batch id 或阶段投影。 - **证据失效**：已记录的验证、Trace、Review 或 checkpoint 不再满足其引用依据时的显式 stale 状态；失效不会被自动当作风险接受。 - **工作区归属**：路径是否属于当前 feature 的用户结论。已观察不等于已归属；未知路径必须通过批量或逐个 interaction 处理。 - **宿主接线健康**：Claude/Codex adapter 在项目级记录的 SessionStart 或 UserPromptSu ([wxy-hh/dev-flow](https://github.com/wxy-hh/dev-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/glossary.md) · ★2)
